### [36] [COCOTree: A Dataset and Benchmark for Open Tree-Structured Visual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22068)
*Junhyub Lee,Seunghun Chae,Hyosu Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文形式化并实现了开放树分解任务，通过自动化标注管道生成COCOTree数据集（2.1万图像、180万结构节点），并提出OTQ评估指标，实现图像到层次化视觉组件树的无约束分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像分割方法缺乏对图像中层次化结构组件的灵活分解能力，人工标注成本极高，亟需一种自动化的开放词汇树结构分解方案。

Method: ① 提出结合LVLMs语义推理与SAM 3几何定位的全自动标注管道；② 构建大规模COCOTree基准数据集（21K图像，180万节点，3500+开放词汇标签）；③ 提出OTQ指标联合评估掩码精度、标签准确性和结构一致性。

Result: 构建了包含21K图像、180万结构节点、超3500个唯一标签的COCOTree数据集，人工评估验证了标注结果与人类结构判断的高度一致性。

Conclusion: 开放树分解任务可行且有效，自动化管道可替代人工标注生成高质量层次化结构数据，为视觉场景理解提供新范式。

Abstract: We formalize and enable the task of open tree decomposition, which segments an image into hierarchical trees of visual components with unconstrained granularity and flexibility. Specifically, we provide the foundation benchmark for this new paradigm with the following three key contributions. First, we overcome the prohibitively high cognitive and physical bottlenecks of manual annotation by developing a fully automated generation pipeline that synergizes the semantic reasoning of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) with the precise geometric grounding of SAM 3. Second, leveraging this pipeline, we construct COCOTree, a massive-scale benchmark featuring over 21K images and 1.8M structural nodes. By embracing an open-vocabulary space of over 3.5K unique labels, it successfully captures the long-tail distribution of complex physical assemblies. Notably, rigorous human evaluation confirms our generated annotations demonstrate strong alignment with human structural judgment. Third, we establish a standardized evaluation protocol by proposing the Open Tree Quality (OTQ) metric, which jointly assesses mask precision, label accuracy, and structural consistency. We release our dataset and benchmark code at https://github.com/melonkick3090/COCOTree.

</details>


### [37] [TWINGS: Thin Plate Splines Warp-aligned Initialization for Sparse-View Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22069)
*Hyeseong Kim,Geonhui Son,Deukhee Lee,Dosik Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TWINGS通过薄板样条（TPS）变形模型对齐反投影点与三角化控制点，为3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）提供快速且几何精确的初始化，从而在稀疏视图输入下实现高质量的新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视图输入下，3D场景重建面临点云稀疏性挑战，现有方法难以在有限视角下保持高质量的结构细节和颜色保真度。

Method: 1）利用薄板样条（TPS）作为平滑非刚性变形模型，通过最小化弯曲能量从控制点对应关系估计全局一致变形；2）将估计深度反投影的点与三角化3D控制点对齐，生成校准后的反投影点；3）在控制点附近采样这些校准点，为3DGS提供快速且几何精确的初始化。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF360数据集上，TWINGS在稀疏视图场景下始终优于现有方法，实现了更精细的结构细节和更准确的色彩重建。

Conclusion: TWINGS通过TPS驱动的几何校准初始化有效缓解了点稀疏性问题，显著提升了稀疏视图下3DGS的重建质量，是一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse-view inputs poses a significant challenge in 3D computer vision, particularly for achieving high-quality scene reconstructions with limited viewpoints. We introduce TWINGS, a framework that enhances 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) by directly addressing point sparsity. We employ Thin Plate Splines (TPS), a smooth non-rigid deformation model that minimizes bending energy to estimate a globally coherent warp from control-point correspondences, to align backprojected points from estimated depth with triangulated 3D control points, yielding calibrated backprojected points. By sampling these calibrated points near the control points, TWINGS provides a fast and geometrically accurate initialization for 3DGS, ultimately improving structural detail preservation and color fidelity in reconstructed scenes. Extensive experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF360 demonstrate that TWINGS consistently outperforms existing methods, delivering detailed and accurate reconstructions under sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [38] [JMed48k: A Multi-Profession Japanese Medical Licensing Benchmark for Vision-Language Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22080)
*Yue Xun,Junyu Liu,Qian Niu,Xinyi Wang,Zheng Yuan,Zirui Li,Zequn Zhang,Bowen Zhao,Shujun Wang,Irene Li,Kan Hatakeyama-Sato,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了JMed48k，一个多职种日本医疗执照考试基准，包含48,862道试题和20,142张图片，涵盖2005-2025年间11个国家执照考试。通过评估21个模型（专有、开源和医疗专用），发现专有和开源模型从图像中获益显著，而医疗专用系统对视觉证据的利用有限。图像移除效应在不同职种间差异可达7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗视觉语言模型评估缺乏多职种、执照级别的日语基准，无法系统评估模型在不同医疗专业领域利用视觉信息的能力。

Method: 从日本厚生劳动省官方PDF资料中构建JMed48k数据集，包含11个职种的国家执照考试题目，图像按8类分类法标注。从中提取近五年子集JMed48k-Eval（12,484道题）。引入配对图像移除审计方法，对比移除图像前后模型的作答状态变化。

Result: 专有和开源模型从图像中获益显著；医疗专用系统对视觉证据利用有限，很多正确答案在移除图像后仍然保持正确。专有模型中，图像移除效应在不同职种间差异巨大（从医师+5.7分到保健师+39.8分）。

Conclusion: JMed48k为视觉语言模型在医疗执照考试场景下提供了可复现、按职种分层的评估基准，揭示了不同模型家族在视觉信息利用方面的显著差异。

Abstract: We introduce JMed48k, a multi-profession Japanese healthcare licensing benchmark for evaluating vision-language models. Built from official PDF materials released by the Japanese Ministry of Health, Labour and Welfare, JMed48k contains 48,862 exam questions and 20,142 images from 11 national licensing examinations between 2005 and 2025, with visual content annotated under an 8-type taxonomy. From this corpus, we derive JMed48k-Eval, a recent five-year evaluation subset with 12,484 scored questions, including 9,905 text-only questions and 2,579 questions with images. We evaluate 21 proprietary, open-source, and medical-specific models, reporting text-only and with-image performance separately. Because these subsets contain different questions, we further introduce a paired image-removal audit that evaluates questions with images before and after removing visual content to explore four answer-transition states. The audit shows that proprietary and open source models gain substantially from images, whereas medical-specific systems show limited observable use of visual evidence, with many correct answers persisting after image removal. Even among proprietary models, the net image-removal effect varies sevenfold across professions, from +5.7 points on Physician questions to +39.8 points on Public Health Nurse questions. We release JMed48k to support reproducible, profession-stratified evaluation of vision-language models in medical licensing settings.

</details>


### [39] [GenHAR: Generalizing Cross-domain Human Activity Recognition for Last-mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22086)
*Zhiqing Hong,Zelong Li,Xiubin Fan,Guang Yang,Baoshen Guo,Haotian Wang,Tian He,Desheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenHAR是一个通过学习域不变传感器表示来提升人类活动识别（HAR）跨域泛化能力的框架。它通过对传感器数据进行分词、学习频率通道相关性、选择性掩码和高效注意力机制，在真实数据集上实现了9.97%的准确率提升和6.4倍计算量降低，并在4个城市部署检测了21.5亿次实时活动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR方法面临不同传感器数据域之间的分布偏移问题，导致在真实应用场景中性能下降。

Method: GenHAR框架包含两大创新：(1) 对传感器数据进行分词，学习频率传感器通道维度间的相关性；(2) 通过选择性掩码和高效注意力机制提升模型效率。

Result: 相比最先进方法，准确率提升9.97%，浮点运算量减少6.4倍；在4个城市的物流公司部署，检测了21.5亿次实时活动。

Conclusion: GenHAR能有效缓解传感器数据域分布偏移问题，实现高效且鲁棒的跨域HAR，在实际部署中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) has shown remarkable effectiveness in various applications, such as smart healthcare and intelligent manufacturing. However, a major challenge faced by HAR is the distribution shift across different sensor data domains, which often leads to decreased performance when deployed for real-world applications. To address this issue, this paper introduces GenHAR, a novel framework designed to mitigate the domain gap by learning domain-invariant sensor representations. GenHAR aims to enhance the generalization capabilities of HAR on target domains purely with data from the source domain. The key novelty of GenHAR lies in two aspects. Firstly, GenHAR tokenizes sensor data and learns correlations among frequency sensor channel dimensions to improve the robustness of HAR models. Secondly, GenHAR improves the efficiency via selective masking and an efficient attention mechanism. We conduct a systematic analysis of GenHAR by comparing it with state-of-the-art HAR methods on real-world human activity datasets. Results show that GenHAR outperforms state-of-the-art methods by 9.97% in accuracy, and reduces Floating Point Operations by 6.4 times. Moreover, we deploy GenHAR at a leading logistics company in 4 cities, and have detected 2.15 billion real-time activities. We release our code at: https://github.com/Sensor-FoundationModel/GenHAR.

</details>


### [40] [LVDrive: Latent Visual Representation Enhanced Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22089)
*Xiaodong Mei,Diankun Zhang,Hongwei Xie,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LVDrive提出了一种基于潜在视觉表示增强的VLA框架，通过在未来潜在空间中预测场景表示而非像素级重建，并采用单次前向传播联合建模未来场景与运动预测，以及两阶段轨迹解码策略，显著提升了端到端自动驾驶的闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖稀疏的动作监督，未充分利用其强大的场景理解与推理能力；而引入稠密视觉监督的世界模型方法又过度强调像素级图像重建，忽略了语义上有意义的场景表示学习。

Method: 1) 在VLA范式中引入未来场景预测任务，表示在高级潜在空间中学习，并借助预训练视觉骨干的辅助监督；2) 在统一嵌入空间中联合建模未来场景与运动预测，通过单次前向传播实现未来感知推理；3) 设计两阶段轨迹解码策略，显式利用学习的潜在未来表示来优化轨迹生成。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准上的广泛实验表明，LVDrive在闭环驾驶性能上取得了显著提升，优于纯动作监督方法和基于图像重建的世界模型方法。

Conclusion: LVDrive证明了利用潜在空间中的未来场景表示学习可以有效增强VLA模型的自动驾驶能力，避免了像素级重建的冗余，同时提供了比稀疏动作监督更丰富的语义指导。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising framework for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing VLAs typically rely on sparse action supervision, which underutilizes their powerful scene understanding and reasoning capabilities. Recent attempts to incorporate dense visual supervision via world modeling often overemphasize pixel-level image reconstruction, neglecting semantically meaningful scene representation learning. In this work, we propose LVDrive, a Latent Visual representation enhanced VLA framework for autonomous driving. LVDrive introduces a future scene prediction task into the VLA paradigm, where future representations are learned entirely in a high-level latent space under auxiliary supervision from a pretrained vision backbone. Departing from inefficient autoregressive generation, we jointly model future scene and motion prediction within a unified embedding space, processed in a single forward pass to conduct the future-aware reasoning. We further design a two-stage trajectory decoding strategy that explicitly leverages the learned latent future representations to refine trajectory generation. Extensive experiments on the challenging Bench2Drive benchmark demonstrate that LVDrive achieves significant improvements in closed-loop driving performance, outperforming both action supervised methods and image-reconstruction-based world model approaches.

</details>


### [41] [VISTA: Validation-Guided Integration of Spatial and Temporal Foundation Models with Anatomical Decoding for Rare-Pathology VCE Event Detection -- after competition results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22096)
*Bo-Cheng Qiu,Fang-Ying Lin,Ming-Han Sun,Yu-Fan Lin,Chia-Ming Lee,Chih-Chung Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向胶囊内镜事件检测的度量对齐多骨干框架，结合EndoFM-LV和DINOv3两个基础模型，通过多头集成、验证引导加权融合和解剖感知时序解码实现事件级检测，在RAREVISION竞赛中取得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶囊内镜事件检测面临三大挑战：临床相关发现稀疏、视觉异质性高、评估标准为事件级别而非帧级别准确率。现有方法难以有效对齐模型优化目标与事件级评估指标。

Method: VISTA框架包含三个核心组件：（1）Diverse Head Ensemble (DHE)：多头部集成；（2）Validation-Guided Weighted Fusion (VGWF)：验证引导的加权融合；（3）Anatomy-Aware Temporal Event Decoding (ATED)：解剖感知时序事件解码。基础模型采用EndoFM-LV提取时序上下文和DINOv3 ViTL/16提取帧级视觉语义。

Result: 官方提交在隐藏测试集上获得temporal mAP@0.5为0.3530、mAP@0.95为0.3235。赛后通过局部阈值精炼结合全局粗搜索优化，性能提升至mAP@0.5为0.3726、mAP@0.95为0.3431，最终排名第二。

Conclusion: VISTA通过度量对齐的多骨干框架有效解决了胶囊内镜事件检测中稀疏事件与帧级评估不匹配的问题，全局粗搜索与局部阈值精炼的组合策略能进一步提升检测性能。

Abstract: Capsule endoscopy event detection is challenging because clinically relevant findings are sparse, visually heterogeneous, and evaluated at the event level rather than by frame accuracy. We propose VISTA, a metric-aligned multi-backbone framework for the RAREVISION task. VISTA combines EndoFM-LV for temporal context and DINOv3 ViTL/16 for frame-level visual semantics, followed by a Diverse Head Ensemble (DHE), Validation-Guided Weighted Fusion (VGWF), and Anatomy-Aware Temporal Event Decoding (ATED). The original official submission achieved hidden-test temporal mAP@0.5 of 0.3530 and mAP@0.95 of 0.3235. After the competition, extending local threshold refinement with a global coarse search improved performance to 0.3726 mAP@0.5 and 0.3431 mAP@0.95, ranking Team ACVLab second in the post-competition evaluation.

</details>


### [42] [OPERA: An Agent for Image Restoration with End-to-End Joint Planning-Execution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22104)
*Feng Zhu,Shuyang Xie,Yihan Zeng,Ming Liu,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OPERA框架，通过强化学习优化规划空间和联合训练工具执行，端到端解决多退化图像恢复中的组合规划与工具协作问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的方法受限于隐式约束的规划空间以及独立预训练工具之间缺乏协调，导致在多退化图像恢复任务中性能受限。

Method: 规划侧使用强化学习直接优化组合规划空间，以最终恢复质量为奖励；执行侧引入智能体引导的联合训练，使恢复工具在序列化组合下学习协作行为。

Result: 在多退化基准和真实数据集上，OPERA在复杂退化场景中一致优于一体化恢复模型和现有基于智能体的方法。

Conclusion: 端到端联合优化规划与执行可有效突破隐式规划限制，实现工具间的协同，显著提升复杂多退化图像恢复性能。

Abstract: Real-world image restoration is challenging due to complex and interacting mixed degradations. Recent agent-based approaches address this problem by composing multiple task-specific restoration tools. However, empirical analysis reveals that their performance is fundamentally limited by implicitly constrained planning spaces and the lack of coordination among independently pretrained tools. To address these issues, we propose OPERA (Optimized Planning-Execution Restoration Agent), a framework that jointly optimizes restoration planning and tool execution in an end-to-end manner. On the planning side, OPERA uses reinforcement learning to directly optimize tool composition over a combinatorial plan space, with the final restoration quality as the reward. On the execution side, OPERA introduces agent-guided co-training of restoration tools, enabling them to learn cooperative behaviors under sequential composition. Extensive experiments on multi-degradation benchmarks and real-world datasets demonstrate that OPERA consistently outperforms both all-in-one restoration models and existing agent-based methods across diverse and complex degradation scenarios.

</details>


### [43] [MotionDPS: Motion-Compensated 3D Brain MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22121)
*Antonio Ortiz-Gonzalez,Erich Kobler,Lukas Schletter,Alexander Effland*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的贝叶斯框架，通过预训练3D复数分数扩散模型作为解剖图像先验，联合估计解剖图像、刚体运动参数和线圈敏感度图，实现无监督的运动补偿3D MRI重建，在严重运动和高速加速场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI对患者运动高度敏感，传统回顾性运动补偿在加速采集中因联合重建与运动估计的病态问题而极具挑战性。

Method: 构建统一的贝叶斯框架，集成预训练3D复数分数扩散模型作为图像先验，通过交替扩散后验图像更新与近端优化步骤估计运动和线圈敏感度，实现无监督重建。

Result: 在模拟和真实运动脑MRI数据集上，该方法在严重运动和高速加速条件下相比经典和学习式运动校正方法取得了更优的图像质量和运动鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯框架有效解决了运动补偿MRI中联合重建与估计的病态问题，无需配对无运动训练数据即可实现高质量重建。

Abstract: Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is highly susceptible to patient motion due to its relatively long acquisition times and the fact that data are acquired sequentially in k-space. Even small patient movements introduce phase inconsistencies across measurements, leading to severe artifacts such as blurring, ghosting, and geometric distortions that can compromise diagnostic quality. Retrospective motion compensation remains challenging, particularly in accelerated acquisitions, due to the ill-posed nature of the joint reconstruction and motion estimation problem. In this work, we propose a unified Bayesian framework for motion-compensated 3D MRI that jointly estimates the anatomical image, rigid-body motion parameters, and coil sensitivity maps directly from motion-corrupted k-space data. Our approach integrates pretrained 3D complex-valued score-based diffusion models as expressive anatomical image priors within a physics-based forward model. Inference is performed by alternating diffusion posterior image updates with efficient proximal optimization steps for motion and coil sensitivity estimation, enabling fully unsupervised reconstruction without the need for paired motion-free training data. Experiments on simulated and real-motion brain MRI datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves improved image quality and motion robustness compared to state-of-the-art classical and learning-based motion correction techniques, particularly in the presence of severe motion and high acceleration.

</details>


### [44] [AesFormer: Transform Everyday Photos into Beautiful Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22126)
*Tianxiang Du,Hulingxiao He,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AesFormer，一种两阶段框架，通过解耦美学规划与图像编辑，实现照片的结构性美学重建（APR），在保留主体身份和场景语义的前提下提升照片美学质量。第一阶段AesThinker分析输入图像并输出可执行编辑动作（引入GRPO-A促进多样化探索），第二阶段AesEditor根据动作指导进行结构性编辑。同时构建了包含9,071对严格对齐的(poor, good)图像对的AesRecon基准。实验表明AesFormer显著提升APR性能，与Nano Banana Pro相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常摄影中，即使捕捉到美学上吸引人的瞬间，照片仍常存在结构性缺陷（如构图、视角、姿势），现有修图和肖像增强方法无法修复这些缺陷。目标是通过结构性重建提升照片美学质量，同时保留主体身份和场景语义。

Method: 提出AesFormer两阶段框架：(1) Stage 1: AesThinker美学动作模型，沿7个渐进式摄影维度分析输入图像，输出可执行编辑动作，并引入GRPO-A（基于GRPO的变体）促进动作空间的广泛探索，超越SFT；(2) Stage 2: AesEditor动作条件编辑器，根据动作指导进行结构性编辑。同时构建基于视频的语料挖掘管道(VCMP)和AesRecon基准（9,071对严格对齐的(poor, good)图像对）。

Result: AesFormer显著提升了APR性能，并与Nano Banana Pro达到竞争力水平。

Conclusion: 通过解耦美学规划与图像编辑的两阶段设计，AesFormer有效提升了照片的结构性美学重建效果，证明了美学理解在大规模图像编辑中的重要性。

Abstract: In everyday photography, aesthetically appealing moments are often captured with structural flaws (e.g., composition, camera viewpoint, or pose) that existing retouching and portrait enhancement methods cannot fix. We formulate Aesthetic Photo Reconstruction (APR) as improving a photo's aesthetic quality via structural reconstruction while preserving subject identity and scene semantics. Although recent advances in image editing models make APR feasible, they often lack aesthetic understanding, yielding edits that are semantically plausible yet aesthetically weak. To address this, we propose AesFormer, a two-stage framework that decouples aesthetic planning from image editing. In Stage 1, an aesthetic action model (AesThinker) analyzes the input along seven progressive photographic dimensions and outputs executable editing actions; we further apply GRPO-A to encourage broad exploration over diverse action plans beyond SFT. In Stage 2, an action-conditioned editor (AesEditor) performs structural edits guided by these actions. To support APR, we build a video-based corpus-mining pipeline (VCMP) and construct AesRecon, a benchmark of 9,071 strictly aligned (poor, good) image pairs. Experiments show that AesFormer substantially improves APR performance and is competitive with Nano Banana Pro. Code is available at https://github.com/PKU-ICST-MIPL/AesFormer_ICML2026.

</details>


### [45] [Accelerating Vision Foundation Models with Drop-in Depthwise Convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22132)
*Carmelo Scribano,Mohammad Mahdi,Nedyalko Prisadnikov,Yuqian Fu,Giorgia Franchini,Danda Pani Paudel,Marko Bertogna,Luc Van Gool*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过利用ViT中部分注意力头表现出的类卷积行为，将其替换为高效的深度可分离卷积层，在保持特征提取能力的同时实现17-20%的推理加速，并通过微调恢复下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练视觉Transformer（ViT）推理成本高，难以部署在资源受限设备上，需要在不严重损失特征提取能力的前提下加速ViT推理。

Method: 1) 识别ViT中表现类卷积行为的注意力头；2) 将这些注意力头替换为高效的深度可分离卷积层（作为即插即用替代）；3) 通过微调恢复下游任务性能。

Result: 在图像分类和分割任务上实现17-20%的推理加速，性能下降极小。

Conclusion: 通过识别并替换类卷积注意力头为深度可分离卷积，可以在显著加速ViT的同时保持其表示能力，为预训练视觉基础模型在资源受限设备上的部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Pretrained vision foundation models deliver strong performance across tasks with limited fine-tuning. However, their Vision Transformer (ViT) backbones impose high inference costs, limiting deployment on resource-constrained devices. In this work, we accelerate large-scale pretrained ViTs while preserving their feature extraction capabilities by exploiting the intrinsic convolution-like behavior of some attention heads. Specifically, we introduce an efficient depthwise convolution-based layer that serves as a drop-in replacement for these heads. Additionally, we propose simple strategies to identify which heads can be replaced and introduce a fine-tuning procedure that recovers downstream task performance. Across both image classification and segmentation tasks, our method achieves 17-20\% percent inference speedup with minimal performance degradation. We validate the approach through detailed derivations, extensive experiments, and efficiency benchmarks. The reference implementation is publicly available.

</details>


### [46] [One Sentence, One Drama: Personalized Short-Form Drama Generation via Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22144)
*Yufei Shi,Weilong Yan,Naixuan Huang,Yucheng Chen,Chenyu Zhang,Tao He,Si Yong Yeo,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"One Sentence, One Drama"框架，一个层次化多智能体系统，能将用户单句创意转化为完整短剧，通过多智能体辩论生成、3D空间一致性机制和多阶段审查循环，解决了现有方法在叙事节奏、空间一致性和制作质量控制方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字短剧制作方法依赖LLM一次性生成剧本和松散耦合的流水线，无法满足三个关键需求：叙事节奏（缺乏钩子、升级不足、结局平淡）、空间一致性（场景布局漂移、角色位置不一致）、制作级质量控制（需大量人工审查修正）。

Method: 提出层次化多智能体框架，包含三大核心组件：(1) 多智能体辩论式故事生成模块，强制执行短剧节奏和叙事连贯性；(2) 基于3D首帧生成机制，建立共享空间参考以确保角色位置和场景布局一致；(3) 多阶段审查循环，在剧本、视觉和视频生成阶段进行错误检测和定向修正。同时引入场景级BGM匹配和场景过渡规划提升沉浸体验。

Result: 引入Short-Drama-Bench基准测试，在标准视频质量指标基础上扩展短剧特定评估标准。实验结果表明该方法在叙事质量、跨片段一致性和整体观看体验上显著优于现有流水线。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了短剧生成中的叙事节奏、空间一致性和质量控制三大关键挑战，为数字短剧自动化生产提供了可行的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Existing approaches for digital short-drama production typically rely on one-shot LLM generated scripts and loosely coupled pipelines, which fail to satisfy three key requirements of short-drama generation: (1) narrative pacing, resulting in weak hooks, insufficient escalation, and unattractive endings; (2) spatial consistency, leading to drifting scene layouts and inconsistent character positions across clips; and (3) production-level quality control, requiring extensive manual review and correction across script and visual stages. We present One Sentence, One Drama, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that transforms a user's single-sentence idea into a fully produced short drama through structured intermediate modules and iterative refinement. Our approach is built upon three key components: (1) a multi-agent debate-based story generation module that enforces short-drama pacing and narrative coherence; (2) a 3D-grounded first-frame generation mechanism that establishes a shared spatial reference for consistent character positioning and scene layout across clips; and (3) multi-stage reviewer loops that perform comprehensive error detection and targeted revision across script, visual, and video generation stages. We also introduce scene-level BGM matching and scene transition planning to improve the audience's immersive experience. To systematically evaluate this task, we introduce Short-Drama-Bench, a benchmark that extends standard video quality metrics with short-drama-specific criteria. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing pipelines in narrative quality, cross-clip consistency, and overall viewing experience.

</details>


### [47] [Flow-based Gaussian Splatting for Continuous-Scale Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22147)
*Jiangwei Mo,Xi Lu,Hanlin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowGS提出了一种基于流匹配和二维高斯溅射的生成式超分辨率框架，用于遥感图像的任意尺度超分辨率重建，在保持竞争性感知质量的同时显著提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率遥感图像获取受传感器限制和成本制约，现有扩散模型超分辨率方法推理步骤多（40-1000步），且在连续尺度超分辨率设置下灵活性有限。

Method: FlowGS通过流匹配（flow matching）结合快捷一致性约束（shortcut consistency），建模高低分辨率图像间的高频细节表示，学习从噪声到细节先验的连续概率流；同时利用二维高斯溅射（2D Gaussian splatting）构建连续特征场，实现任意查询位置的重建。

Result: 在连续尺度和固定尺度超分辨率设置下，FlowGS均取得了与现有方法相当的竞争性感知质量，并显著提升了推理效率。

Conclusion: FlowGS有效解决了生成式超分辨率方法推理效率低和连续尺度灵活性不足的问题，为遥感图像任意尺度超分辨率提供了一种高效且高质量的生成式重建框架。

Abstract: High-resolution remote sensing images (RSIs) are crucial for Earth observation applications, yet acquiring them is often limited by sensor constraints and costs. In recent years, generative super-resolution (SR) methods, particularly diffusion models, have made significant progress. However, they typically require slow iterative inference with 40--1000 steps and exhibit limited flexibility in continuous-scale SR settings. To address these issues, we propose FlowGS, a generative reconstruction framework for arbitrary-scale SR of RSIs. FlowGS models the high-frequency detail representations between high- and low-resolution images and learns a continuous probability flow from noise to detail priors via flow matching (FM) constrained by shortcut consistency, thereby reducing generative complexity and improving inference efficiency. Additionally, we employ 2D Gaussian splatting to construct a continuous feature field, thereby enabling flexible reconstruction at arbitrary query locations. Experimental results show that FlowGS delivers competitive perceptual quality compared with existing methods in both continuous-scale and fixed-scale SR settings, with substantially improved inference efficiency.

</details>


### [48] [Balancing Uncertainty and Diversity of Samples: Leveraging Diversity of Least, High Confidence Samples for Effective Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22169)
*Vipul Arya,S. H. Shabbeer Basha,Srikrishna U N,Sunainha Vijay,Snehasis Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出四种新型混合采样方法（LCD等）用于主动学习，结合不确定性与多样性策略，实验表明LCD方法优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习方法仅基于单一维度（多样性或不确定性）选择样本，可能限制模型性能。本文旨在通过混合采样策略同时捕捉易/难样本的多样性与不确定性，提升模型学习效果。

Method: 提出四种混合采样方法，其中核心方法LCD（Least Confident and Diverse）结合最低置信度（不确定性）与多样性准则，从无标签池中同时选取高/低置信度且多样化的样本进行标注。

Result: 实验表明LCD方法在多种任务中持续优于现有方法；选取不确定且多样化的实例有助于模型学习更独特的特征。

Conclusion: 混合采样（不确定性+多样性）能有效提升主动学习性能，LCD方法为数据高效的深度学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models, including Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs), have achieved state-of-the-art performance on various computer vision tasks such as object classification, detection, segmentation, generation, and many more. However, these models are data-hungry as they require more training data to learn millions or billions of parameters. Especially for supervised learning tasks, curating a large number of labeled samples for model training is an expensive and time-consuming task. Active Learning (AL) has been used to address this problem for many years. Existing active learning methods aim at choosing the samples for annotation from a pool of unlabeled samples that are either diverse or uncertain. Choosing such samples may hinder the model's performance as we pool based on one dimension, i.e., either diverse or uncertain. In this paper, we propose four novel hybrid sampling methods for pooling both easy and hard samples, which are also diverse. To verify the efficacy of the proposed methods, extensive experiments are conducted using high and low-confidence samples separately. We observe from our experiments that the proposed hybrid sampling method, Least Confident and Diverse (LCD), consistently performs better compared to state-of-the-art methods. It is observed that selecting uncertain and diverse instances helps the model learn more distinct features. The codes related to this study will be available at https://github.com/XXX/LCD.

</details>


### [49] [Enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models for Safety-Critical Driving Video Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22185)
*Tomaso Trinci,Henrique Piñeiro Monteagudo,Leonardo Taccari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合下采样视频帧、高频IMU/GPS遥测数据及专用视觉模型语义信息的多模态流水线，为MLLM生成高质量伪标签（描述性字幕和问答对），用于训练MLLM识别和描述驾驶中的安全关键事件。在QwenVL-2.5上微调（DoRA适配器，<50M可训练参数）效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLM在通用视觉理解上表现优异，但应用于安全关键驾驶场景（如碰撞或近碰撞等罕见高风险动态事件）时，缺乏准确的感知和推理能力。

Method: 构建流水线融合三类数据：（1）下采样视频帧；（2）高频遥测数据（IMU和GPS）；（3）专用CV模型提取的语义信息。利用该多模态数据生成高质量伪标签（描述性字幕和QA对），通过DoRA适配器微调QwenVL-2.5模型。

Result: 在少于5000万可训练参数和有限计算预算下，模型在识别和解释安全关键事件方面取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 融合遥测数据和专用视觉模型语义能够有效增强MLLM对驾驶安全关键事件的感知与推理能力，且轻量化微调足以实现显著提升。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in general visual understanding. However, their application to safety-critical driving scenarios remains limited by an inability to accurately perceive and reason about rare high-stakes dynamic events, such as collisions or near-collisions. To address this, we introduce a pipeline that enhances MLLM perception by fusing downsampled video frames with synchronized high-frequency telematics data (IMU and GPS) and semantic insights from specialized computer vision models. Our pipeline generates high-quality pseudo-labels, including descriptive captions and question-answer pairs, specifically designed to train MLLMs to identify and describe Safety-Critical Events (SCEs) in real-world driving footage. We show the effectiveness of our approach fine-tuning the open-source QwenVL-2.5 model via DoRA adapters: our experiments demonstrate significant improvements in identifying and explaining safety-critical events, with fewer than 50M trainable parameters and limited computational budget.

</details>


### [50] [Event-Illumination Collaborative Low-light Image Enhancement with a High-resolution Real-world Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22186)
*Senyan Xu,Zhijing Sun,Kean Liu,Xin Lu,Ruixuan Jiang,Mingyang Huang,Xueyang Fu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EIC-LIE框架，通过事件-光照协同交互模块（EICI）和光照感知事件滤波器（IAEF）解决低光图像增强中忽视全局光照信息和事件信号噪声敏感的问题，并构建了首个高分辨率真实世界事件-图像对数据集，在五个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于事件的低光图像增强方法主要关注利用事件的高动态范围信息，但忽视了图像中关键的全局光照信息以及事件信号在真实场景中固有的噪声敏感性。

Method: 提出EIC-LIE框架，包含：(1) Event-Illumination Collaborative Interaction (EICI)模块，内含前向汇聚（在不同光照条件下汇聚HDR特征）和反向注入（为光照和事件表示提供互补内容）；(2) Illumination-aware Event Filter (IAEF)，基于图像亮度统计动态降低事件噪声；(3) 基于分束器的混合成像系统，用于采集高分辨率真实世界动态场景的事件-图像对数据集。

Result: 在五个真实世界和合成数据集上，EIC-LIE显著超越现有方法，PSNR提升高达1.24dB，SSIM提升0.069。

Conclusion: EIC-LIE通过事件与光照信息的协同交互以及光照感知的事件降噪策略，有效提升了低光图像增强的性能，且提供的数据集和代码将促进该领域发展。

Abstract: Event-based low-light image enhancement (LIE) methods mainly focus on incorporating high dynamic range (HDR) information from events while overlooking the essential global illumination in images and the inherent noise sensitivity of event signals in real-world scenarios. To address these issues, we propose EIC-LIE, an event-illumination collaborative LIE framework. Concretely, we first design an Event-Illumination Collaborative Interaction (EICI) module, which contains two key processes: forward gathering, which gathers HDR features across varying lighting conditions, and backward injection, which provides complementary content for illumination and event representations. Next, we introduce an Illumination-aware Event Filter (IAEF) that dynamically reduces event noise based on brightness statistics derived from images. Additionally, we build a beam-splitter-based hybrid imaging system to collect high-quality event-image pairs with temporal synchronization from dynamic scenes, providing the first high-resolution, real-world event-based LIE dataset. Extensive experiments show that our EIC-LIE outperforms state-of-the-art methods on five real-world and synthetic datasets, significantly surpassing previous methods with improvements of up to 1.24dB in PSNR and 0.069 in SSIM. The code and dataset are released at https://github.com/QUEAHREN/EIC-LIE.

</details>


### [51] [Ultra-High-Definition Image Quality Assessment via Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22192)
*Shaode Yu,Enqi Chen,Ming Huang,Xuemin Ren,Songnan Zhao,Zhicheng Zhang,Qiurui Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UHD-GCN-BIQA框架，通过图表示学习建模超高清图像区域间的结构依赖关系，而非将裁剪块视为独立视图，从而解决UHD图像盲质量评估中尺度敏感性失真和局部-全局关系弱化的问题。在UHD-IQA基准上达到PLCC=0.7784, SRCC=0.8019, RMSE=0.0519，RMSE为最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD图像的盲质量评估面临计算成本高（原生分辨率推理）、尺度敏感失真（暴力缩放或孤立裁剪会抑制）以及局部伪影与全局场景上下文关系弱化三大挑战，亟需一种能有效建模区域间结构依赖关系的方法。

Method: (1) 从每张UHD图像中采样保持宽高比的图像块作为图节点；(2) 利用空间邻近性和特征相似性构建混合k近邻图；(3) 采用残差图卷积传播跨区域的上下文信息；(4) 通过门控注意力池化将块级证据聚合为图像级质量预测；(5) 使用指数移动平均归一化多目标损失函数联合优化回归、相关性和排序三个目标。

Result: 在UHD-IQA基准上，UHD-GCN-BIQA的PLCC=0.7784, SRCC=0.8019, RMSE=0.0519，相关性指标具有竞争力，且RMSE在所有对比方法中最低。

Conclusion: 基于图的区域关系建模对UHD图像质量评估有效，尤其在改善高分辨率视觉内容下绝对质量分数估计方面表现出色。

Abstract: Blind image quality assessment (BIQA) for ultrahighdefinition (UHD) images remains challenging because native-resolution inference is computationally expensive, whereas aggressive resizing or isolated cropping may suppress scale-sensitive distortions and weaken the relationship between local artifacts and global scene context. This paper aims to improve UHD-BIQA by explicitly modeling the structural dependencies among sampled image regions rather than treating them as independent views, and a graph representation learning framework UHD-GCN-BIQA is proposed. The framework samples aspect-ratio-aligned patches from each UHD image, encodes them as graph nodes, and constructs a hybrid k-nearest-neighbor graph using spatial proximity and feature similarity. Residual graph convolution is used to propagate contextual information across regions, and gated attention pooling aggregates patchlevel evidence into an imagelevel quality prediction. An exponential moving average normalized multiobjective loss function is adopted to stabilize the joint optimization of regression, correlation, and ranking objectives. Experiments on the UHD-IQA benchmark show that UHD-GCN-BIQA achieves PLCC = 0.7784, SRCC = 0.8019, and RMSE = 0.0519, obtaining competitive correlation performance and the lowest RMSE among the compared methods. These results indicate that graph-based region relation modeling is effective for UHD image quality assessment, particularly for improving absolute quality score estimation under high-resolution visual content.

</details>


### [52] [OSS: Open Suturing Skills Vision-Based Assessment Challenge 2024-2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22200)
*Hanna Hoffmann,Setareh Bady,Claas de Boer,Max Kirchner,Jan Egger,Rainer Röhrig,Frank Hölzle,Lennart Johannes Gruber,Kunpeng Xie,Marlon Neuhaus,Victor Alves,Guilherme Barbosa,Leonardo Barroso,João Carvalho,Hao Chen,Gabriella d'Albenzio,André Ferreira,Nuno Gomes,Yuichiro Hayashi,Kousuke Hirasawa,Rebecca Hisey,Seungjae Hong,Seoi Jeong,Tiago Jesus,Daehong Kang,Satoshi Kasai,Shunsuke Kikuchi,Takayuki Kitasaka,Satoshi Kondo,Hyoun-Joong Kong,Youngbin Kong,Atsushi Kouno,Shlomi Laufer,Kyu Eun Lee,Bining Long,Nooshin Maghsoodi,Hiroki Matsuzaki,Evangelos Mazomenos,Ori Meiraz,Kensaku Mori,Marina Music,Masahiro Oda,Roi Papo,Jieun Park,Rafael Piexoto,Saeid Rezaei,Mariana Ribeiro,Soyeon Shin,Yang Shu,Idan Smoller,Danail Stoyanov,Yihui Wang,Xinkai Zhao,Sebastian Bodenstedt,Isabel Funke,Stefanie Speidel,Behrus Hinrichs-Puladi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MICCAI OSS挑战赛的成果，旨在通过视频数据对开放式手术技能进行自动化评估。挑战赛包含技能水平分类、OSATS评分预测及手部/工具追踪三个任务。通用时空视频模型表现最佳，但细粒度OSATS评分和关键点追踪仍面临挑战。研究揭示了视频评估在开放手术中的潜力与当前局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高水平手术技能对患者预后至关重要，自动化数据驱动的技能评估有望改善手术培训。虽然机器学习在微创手术技能评估中日益普及，但在开放手术中的应用仍然有限。本文通过组织专门挑战赛来推动这一领域的发展。

Method: 基于MICCAI挑战赛框架，使用静态GoPro摄像机在干实验室环境中录制开放缝合训练任务视频，提供器械轨迹作为辅助模态。挑战赛持续两年，包含三个独立任务：(1)四类技能水平分类、(2)八类别OSATS评分预测、(3)手部与手术工具追踪。参赛者提交了深度学习视频模型、追踪驱动方法和混合方法等多种解决方案。

Result: 通用时空视频模型在各项任务中表现最稳定且最强；概念上多样化的方法在良好执行时也能达到竞争水平。细粒度OSATS评分预测仍具挑战性，但增加训练数据能显著提升效果。由于频繁的遮挡和出框问题，关键点追踪效果不佳，限制了其在基于运动分析中的应用。

Conclusion: 本研究为开放式手术技能评估的自动化方法提供了基准，展示了视频评估的潜力与当前局限，并指出了推动自动技能评估向临床转化的重要方向。

Abstract: Achieving high levels of surgical skill through effective training is essential for optimal patient outcomes. Automated, data-driven skill assessment holds significant potential to improve surgical training. While machine learning-based methods are increasingly popular for assessing skills in minimally invasive surgery, their application to open surgery remains limited. We present the results of a dedicated MICCAI challenge designed to benchmark and advance vision-based skill assessment in open surgery.
  The challenge dataset comprises videos of an open suturing training task recorded with a static GoPro camera in a dry-lab setting, with instrument trajectories available in addition to the primary video modality. The OSS Challenge was hosted over two consecutive years, comprising two and three independent tasks, respectively: (1) classifying skill level into four classes, (2) predicting the full Objective Structured Assessment of Technical Skills across eight categories, and (3) tracking hands and surgical tools. Participants submitted diverse solutions including deep learning-based video models, tracking-driven methods, and hybrid approaches.
  General-purpose spatiotemporal video models consistently achieved the strongest performance, though conceptually diverse approaches reached competitive levels when well-executed. Predicting fine-grained OSATS scores remains challenging but benefits substantially from increased training data. Keypoint tracking proves difficult given frequent occlusions and out-of-frame instances, limiting current applicability for motion-based skill analysis. This work benchmarks innovative and diverse solutions for surgical skill assessment, highlighting both the promise and current limitations of video-based evaluation in open surgery and identifying critical directions for advancing automated skill assessment toward clinical impact.

</details>


### [53] [Zero-Shot Temporal Action Localization Through Textual Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22201)
*Benedetta Liberatori,Alessandro Conti,Lorenzo Vaquero,Paolo Rota,Yiming Wang,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TEGU方法，利用大语言模型和字幕文本增强细粒度动作区分，在无监督的零样本时序动作定位任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在细粒度动作分类上存在困难，监督训练方法泛化性能有限，需要无需标注数据且能提升细粒度动作定位能力的方法。

Method: 利用大语言模型生成的丰富文本信息以及从字幕中提取的结构化文本，为视频中的动作提供更精细的语义线索，从而在无需训练数据的条件下提升零样本时序动作定位性能。

Result: 在THUMOS14和ActivityNet-v1.3数据集上，TEGU方法优于现有的无训练零样本时序动作定位方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入大语言模型和字幕文本的丰富语义信息，可以在无监督条件下有效提升零样本时序动作定位的细粒度判别能力。

Abstract: Zero-shot temporal action localization (ZS-TAL) consists of classifying and localizing actions in untrimmed videos, where action classes are unseen at training time. Existing work uses Vision and Language Models (VLMs), taking advantage of their strong zero-shot transfer capabilities. Yet, these models face evident challenges with fine-grained action classification, making it difficult to directly use them to distinguish between the presence and absence of an action. Most current methods for ZS-TAL address these challenges by training models on large-scale video datasets, which require annotated data and often result in limited generalization performance. Recently, approaches discarding the use of labeled data have emerged as an alternative. Following this direction, we propose a novel approach, ``Textual Guidance for finer localization of actions in videos'' (TEGU), that compensates for the lack of supervision from training data by exploiting rich textual information derived from large language models and structured text extracted from captions. This additional linguistic context can improve fine-grained discrimination by providing richer cues about fine-grained action differences within videos. We validate the effectiveness of the proposed method by conducting experiments on the THUMOS14 and the ActivityNet-v1.3 datasets. Our results show that, by exploiting rich textual information for improved action localization, TEGU outperforms state-of-the-art ZS-TAL approaches that do not involve training

</details>


### [54] [EvoIR-Agent: Self-Evolving Image Restoration Agentic System via Experience-Driven Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22208)
*Kailin Zhuang,Jiawei Wu,Zhi Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoIR-Agent，通过构建分层经验池和自进化机制，解决了MLLM驱动的图像恢复智能体中训练方法（高效但不兼容新工具/退化）与无训练方法（兼容但经验低效）之间的困境，在性能与效率上达到Pareto最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM驱动的图像恢复智能体存在零样本规划失败率高、试错开销大的问题。训练方法虽高效但无法兼容新工具或退化类型，无训练方法虽兼容但经验浅层仍需大量试错，两种范式存在根本性困境。

Method: 首先系统形式化无训练图像恢复智能体的经验组件，然后构建分层经验池实现从粗到细的多样化工具选择和去除顺序引导，最后引入自进化机制从零开始利用累积记录更新经验池。

Result: 在全参考指标上显著领先，与SOTA方法相比在性能与效率间实现了出色的Pareto最优平衡。

Conclusion: EvoIR-Agent通过经验池的自进化有效地解决了兼容性与效率的困境，验证了训练框架在MLLM驱动图像恢复智能体中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-driven image restoration agent demonstrates effectiveness in degradation coupling scenarios by flexibly selecting tools and determining removal orders. However, their zero-shot planning often fails without experience, necessitating severe trial-and-error overhead to achieve satisfactory outcomes. Currently, two paradigms are employed to address this issue, yet a dilemma persists: Training-based methods embed intrinsic experience into parameters, achieving high inference efficiency but lacking compatibility with new tools or degradation. In contrast, training-free methods utilize explicit experience storage for compatibility but still incur trial-and-error overhead due to naive experience. To resolve the dilemma, we propose EvoIR-Agent, which first systematically formulates the experience components of a training-free image restoration agent. Subsequently, a hierarchical experience pool is constructed, which enables coarse-to-fine guidance for diverse tools and removal orders. Furthermore, a self-evolving mechanism is introduced to update the pool from scratch using accumulated records, thereby greatly improving performance and efficiency. Extensive experiments reveal that EvoIR-Agent achieves a significant lead in the full reference metrics and yields a remarkable Pareto-optimal balance between performance and efficiency compared to the state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [55] [SpaceDG: Benchmarking Spatial Intelligence under Visual Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22536)
*Xiaolong Zhou,Yifei Liu,Ziyang Gong,Jiarui Li,Qiyue Zhao,Muyao Niu,Yuanyuan Gao,Le Ma,Xue Yang,Hongjie Zhang,Zhihang Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpaceDG——首个面向退化感知空间理解的大规模数据集，通过基于物理的退化合成引擎在3DGS渲染中嵌入退化形成过程，模拟9种真实世界视觉退化。评估25个MLLMs发现视觉退化严重损害空间推理能力，而基于SpaceDG的微调能显著提升退化鲁棒性，甚至在退化条件下超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间推理基准大多假设完美视觉输入，忽略了真实部署中常见的运动模糊、弱光、恶劣天气、镜头畸变和压缩伪影等视觉退化，导致MLLMs在实际场景中的空间智能鲁棒性未知。

Method: 1) 构建基于物理的退化合成引擎，将退化形成过程嵌入3DGS渲染流程，模拟9种退化类型；2) 从近1000个室内场景生成约1M QA对；3) 构建人工验证的SpaceDG-Bench，包含1102个问题、11种推理类别和9种退化类型，形成超10K VQA实例。

Result: 评估25个开源和闭源MLLMs表明，视觉退化一致且显著地损害空间推理能力，暴露了关键的鲁棒性缺陷。在SpaceDG上微调能显著提升退化鲁棒性，在退化条件下甚至超越人类表现，且不牺牲干净图像上的性能。

Conclusion: 视觉退化是当前MLLMs空间智能的主要脆弱点，而退化感知训练是提升鲁棒空间智能的有效途径，能够在不影响正常表现的前提下实现退化条件下的性能超越人类。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress in spatial intelligence, yet existing spatial reasoning benchmarks largely assume pristine visual inputs and overlook the degradations that commonly occur in real-world deployment, such as motion blur, low light, adverse weather, lens distortion, and compression artifacts. This raises a fundamental question: how robust is the spatial intelligence of current MLLMs when visual observations are imperfect? To answer this question, we introduce SpaceDG, the first large-scale dataset for degradation-aware spatial understanding. It is constructed with a physically grounded degradation synthesis engine that embeds degradation formation process into 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) rendering, enabling realistic simulation of nine degradation types. The resulting dataset contains approximately 1M QA pairs from nearly 1,000 indoor scenes. We further introduce SpaceDG-Bench, an human-verified benchmark with 1,102 questions spanning 11 reasoning categories and 9 visual degradation types, yielding over 10K VQA instances. Evaluating 25 open- and closed-source MLLMs reveals that visual degradations consistently and substantially impair spatial reasoning, exposing a critical robustness gap. Finally, we show that finetuning on SpaceDG markedly improves degradation robustness and can even surpass human performance under degraded conditions without any performance drop on clean images, highlighting the promise of degradation-aware training for robust spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [56] [GALAR-TemporalNet v2: Anatomy-Guided Dual-Branch Temporal Classification with Bidirectional Mamba and Dual-Graph GCN for Video Capsule Endoscopy -- after competition results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22209)
*Jiye Won,Seangmin Lee,Soon Ki Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GALAR-TemporalNet v2，一种层次化时序模型，用于解决视频胶囊内镜(VCE)中的多标签时序分类问题，包括8个解剖区域定位和9个病理发现检测。通过窗口自注意力、双图GCN、双向Mamba、解剖原型残差路径和帧级GCN跳跃连接等创新设计，在RARE-VISION测试集上取得mAP@0.5为0.3409、mAP@0.95为0.3333的SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频胶囊内镜(VCE)面临多标签时序分类三大核心挑战：极端类别不平衡、长程时序依赖以及病理-解剖纠缠，现有方法难以同时处理这些问题。

Method: 1) 窗口自注意力进行局部建模；2) 双图GCN捕捉全局帧关系；3) 双向Mamba选择性编码边界上下文；4) 解剖原型残差路径解耦病理偏离信号与正常器官外观；5) 帧级GCN跳跃连接稳定视觉混淆的稀有类别训练。v2版本重构病理分支、优化损失函数并扩展后处理。

Result: 竞赛版GALAR-TemporalNet在RARE-VISION测试集上mAP@0.5为0.2644，mAP@0.95为0.2353；v2改进版提升至mAP@0.5为0.3409，mAP@0.95为0.3333，相对提升约29%和42%。

Conclusion: 提出的层次化时序模型有效解决了VCE中的多标签分类难题，通过解剖原型残差路径等设计显著提升了病理检测和解剖区域定位的性能，为消化内镜视频分析提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Video Capsule Endoscopy (VCE) poses a challenging multi-label temporal classification problem, requiring simultaneous localization of 8 anatomical regions and detection of 9 pathological findings across tens of thousands of frames. We present GALAR-TemporalNet v2, a hierarchical temporal model that addresses three core challenges: extreme class imbalance, long-range temporal dependencies, and pathology--anatomy entanglement. Our architecture combines windowed self-attention for local modeling, a Dual-Graph GCN for global frame relationships, and Bidirectional Mamba for selective boundary context encoding. A novel anatomy prototype residual pathway decouples pathological deviation signals from normal organ appearance, and a frame-level GCN skip connection stabilizes training of visually confusable rare classes. The competition version, GALAR-TemporalNet, achieved an overall mAP@0.5 of 0.2644 and mAP@0.95 of 0.2353 on the RARE-VISION test set. Following the competition, the redesigned GALAR-TemporalNet v2 -- incorporating a restructured pathology branch, refined loss functions, and extended post-processing -- improved these results to mAP@0.5 of 0.3409 and mAP@0.95 of 0.3333.

</details>


### [57] [A Robust Semantic Segmentation Pipeline for the CVPR 2026 8th UG2+ Challenge Track 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22216)
*Jinming Chai,Libo Yan,Licheng Jiao,Fang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于半监督学习的语义分割方法，用于CVPR 2026 UG2+挑战赛中的恶劣天气场景分割任务。以UniMatch V2为基线，将退化天气图像视为未标注数据进行半监督训练，并在推理阶段使用测试时增强，仅使用WeatherProof数据集，无需额外数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决恶劣天气条件下语义分割性能退化的问题，充分利用WeatherProof数据集中退化天气图像的数据分布，不依赖外部数据。

Method: 采用UniMatch V2作为基线模型，将退化天气图像作为未标注数据进行半监督训练，充分利用数据分布；推理阶段应用测试时增强（Test-Time Augmentation）提升鲁棒性和分割精度。

Result: 在WeatherProof数据集上取得有效分割结果，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 半监督范式结合测试时增强可以有效应对恶劣天气下的语义分割挑战，无需外部数据即可充分利用目标数据集分布。

Abstract: This report presents our solution for the WeatherProof Dataset Challenge, namely CVPR 2026 8th UG2+ Challenge Track 2: Semantic Segmentation in Adverse Weather. For the semantic segmentation task under adverse weather conditions, we propose a semi-supervised segmentation pipeline. Our method is trained exclusively on the WeatherProof dataset, without using any additional external data. Specifically, we adopt UniMatch V2 as the baseline model and treat all degraded-weather images as unlabeled data for semi-supervised training, thereby fully exploiting the data distribution provided by the challenge. During inference, we further apply test-time augmentation to improve the robustness and segmentation accuracy of the final predictions. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/ylb888/weatherproof-challenge-unimatchv2.

</details>


### [58] [REACH: Hand Pose Estimation from Room Corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22231)
*Shu Nakamura,Ryo Kawahara,Genki Kinoshita,Ryosuke Hirai,Yasutomo Kawanishi,Shohei Nobuhara,Ko Nishino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer的新型3D手部姿态估计器REACH-Net，通过利用手-身体协调性、时间进展和多视角观察，从远处低分辨率、频繁遮挡的固定摄像头画面中准确恢复手部形状和姿态。同时引入了首个大规模室内环境手部姿态数据集REACH，包含50名参与者在日常活动中的准确手部运动数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以从远处固定摄像头（如房间角落）获取的低分辨率、频繁遮挡画面中准确估计3D手部姿态，而准确的手部姿态估计对于"野外"连续人类行为分析至关重要。

Method: 提出REACH-Net，一种基于Transformer的模型：1）通过每视角视觉特征token的关联建模手与身体配置；2）以自回归方式利用时间协调性挖掘时序进展。同时构建REACH数据集，使用隐蔽胸挂摄像头在50名参与者日常活动中采集精确手部形状和姿态标注。

Result: 大量实验（包括与现有方法的对比研究）表明，REACH-Net在远距离3D手部姿态估计任务中达到了高度准确的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作拓宽了3D手部姿态估计的边界，尤其推动了"野外"连续人类行为分析的发展。

Abstract: We introduce a novel 3D hand pose estimator that can accurately recover the shape and pose of people's hands in a room from afar, typically from fixed cameras at room corners, in extremely low-resolution and frequently occluded views. Our key idea is to fully leverage hand-body coordination, its temporal progression, and multiview observations. We achieve this with a novel Transformer-based model, in which hand and body configurations are modeled through correlations between their visual features expressed as per-view tokens, and their temporal coordination is exploited in an autoregressive manner. We introduce a novel dataset, which we refer to as REACH, Room-Environment dataset Annotated with Chest cameras for Hand pose estimation, to train and test our method. REACH is a first-of-its-kind large-scale hand pose dataset that captures accurate hand movements of 50 participants across a wide variety of daily activities. In order to avoid interfering with natural movements while annotating the hands with accurate shape and pose, we leverage concealed chest cameras. Through extensive experiments, including comparative studies with existing methods, we show that our model, REACH-Net, achieves highly accurate 3D hand pose estimation from afar. These results broaden the horizon of 3D hand pose estimation, especially towards "in-the-wild" continuous human behavior analysis.

</details>


### [59] [D3Seg: Dependency-Aware Diffusion for Brain Tumor Segmentation with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22249)
*Danish Ali,Ajmal Mian,Naveed Akhtar,Ghulam Mubashar Hassan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: D3Seg提出了一种多跳模态图融合、轻量扩散插补和概率空间决策修正的方法，在BraTS 2023数据集上缺失模态场景下实现了1.5-2.0%的ET和1.0%的TC Dice提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床中MRI序列缺失会导致传统分割方法性能严重下降，现有方法依赖简单的特征拼接或直接融合策略，无法有效处理缺失模态问题。

Method: 1) 多跳模态图融合(MMGF)建模高阶模态间依赖关系；2) 轻量扩散插补机制在潜在空间补偿缺失的T1ce表征；3) 概率空间决策修正缓解主导类别过度自信并改善肿瘤亚区划分。

Result: 在BraTS 2023上，缺失模态配置下增强肿瘤(ET) Dice提升约1.5-2.0%，肿瘤核心(TC)提升约1.0%，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: D3Seg通过图融合、扩散插补和决策修正三管齐下的策略，有效解决了缺失模态下的脑肿瘤分割性能退化问题。

Abstract: Accurate brain tumor segmentation using multiparametric MRI is critical for effective treatment planning. However, in clinical settings, complete acquisition of all MRI sequences is not always possible. The absence of certain MRI modalities results in substantial performance degradation in existing segmentation methods, which typically rely on naive feature concatenation or direct fusion strategies. To address this limitation, we propose a novel segmentation model D3Seg which is designed to maintain stable performance under missing-modality settings. D3Seg introduces Multi-hop Modality Graph Fusion (MMGF) to model higher order inter-modality dependencies, a lightweight diffusion-based imputation mechanism to compensate for missing T1ce representations in latent space, and probability-space decision refinement to mitigate dominant class overconfidence and improve delineation of underrepresented tumor subregions. Extensive evaluation on BraTS 2023 dataset demonstrates that our D3Seg model consistently improves segmentation performance under missing modality configurations. The proposed model achieves approximately 1.5-2.0% Dice improvement on enhancing tumor (ET) and around 1.0% on tumor core (TC) across multiple missing modality configurations compared to the current state-of-the-art model, while maintaining computational efficiency.

</details>


### [60] [Direct content-based retrieval from music scores images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22255)
*Noelia Luna-Barahona,Antonio Ríos-Vila,David Rizo,Jorge Calvo-Zaragoza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了乐谱图像的内容检索方法，比较了基于光学音乐识别（OMR）的转录方法、无需转录的Transformer模型以及基于文本提示的大语言模型，发现OMR在域内检索更优，而无需转录的方法在跨域泛化上表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐谱数字化虽已普及，但检索仍依赖元数据（如标题、作曲家），基于内容（如旋律片段）的乐谱图像检索研究不足，对音乐家、音乐学家和教育工作者具有重要应用价值。

Method: 首先研究乐谱检索中最相关的特征，并定义从任意标注语料库构建查询数据集的系统方法。然后比较三类方法：基于OMR的转录方法、无需转录的Transformer模型（直接从乐谱图像识别查询）、以及文本提示的大语言模型。在四个不同规模、图像质量和排版机制的语料库上进行评估。

Result: 不同方法在不同条件下各有所长：OMR流水线在域内检索中表现更好，而无需转录的Transformer模型在处理域变异性（跨域泛化）方面更为有效。

Conclusion: 乐谱图像内容检索没有万能方法，需根据具体应用场景（域内高精度或跨域泛化）选择合适的技术路线。

Abstract: The digitization of musical scores plays a crucial role in their preservation and accessibility, yet information retrieval still depends mainly on metadata searches, such as by title or composer. Content based search in music score images remains underexplored compared to text documents, despite its potential value for musicians, musicologists, and educators. This work contributes to the field by first studying which characteristics of a score are most relevant for search and by defining a systematic method to build query datasets from any annotated corpus. We also consider diverse methods for content-based search on music score images, ranging from transcription-based approaches relying on Optical Music Recognition (OMR), to a transcription-free Transformer model trained to recognize queries directly from score images, and a text-prompted Large Language Model. Our experiments evaluate these models on four corpora exhibiting diverse characteristics in terms of dataset size, image quality, and typesetting mechanisms. Overall, each method excels under different conditions: OMR-based pipelines achieve higher in-domain retrieval, whereas transcription-free models handle domain variability more effectively.

</details>


### [61] [MuKV: Multi-Grained KV Cache Compression for Long Streaming Video Question-Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22269)
*Junbin Xiao,Jiajun Chen,Tianxiang Sun,Xun Yang,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuKV提出了一种多粒度KV缓存压缩模块和半层次化检索方法，通过patch、frame、segment三级粒度保留局部细节与全局时序上下文，结合自注意力和频率引导的双信号token压缩机制，实现长流式视频问答的高效与高精度。实验表明，该方法在不牺牲内存和在线问答效率的前提下显著提升答案准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长流式视频问答中，现有KV缓存方法每1-2帧缓存一次，导致冗余内存占用，同时丢失帧内细粒度空间细节和帧间时序上下文，亟需高效且保真的缓存压缩方案。

Method: MuKV包含两部分：1）离线KV缓存模块：在patch、frame、segment三级粒度提取视觉表示，通过自注意力与频率引导的双信号token压缩机制实现高效压缩；2）在线问答模块：采用半层次化检索方法检索相关KV缓存用于答案生成。

Result: 在长流式VideoQA基准上，MuKV显著提升答案准确率，且不牺牲内存和在线问答效率；其压缩机制本身在准确率、内存和问答效率上均持续优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 多粒度KV缓存压缩与半层次化检索能有效平衡长流式视频问答的效率与精度，其中双信号token压缩机制是核心贡献。

Abstract: Long streaming video QA remains challenging due to growing visual tokens and limited reasoning length of large language models (LLMs). KV-caching stores the Key-Value (KV) of the historical tokens via LLM prefill and enables more efficient streaming QA. However, existing methods cache every one or two frames, causing redundant memory usage and losing fine-grained spatial details within frame or temporal contexts across frames. This paper proposes MuKV, a method that features a multi-grained KV cache compression module and a semi-hierarchical retrieval approach to improve both efficiency and accuracy for long streaming VideoQA. For the offline KV cache, MuKV extracts visual representations at patch-, frame-, and segment-levels. The multiple levels of granularity preserve both local cues and global temporal context, while maintaining efficiency with a dual signal token compression mechanism guided by self-attention and frequency. For online QA, MuKV designs a semi-hierarchical retrieval method to retrieve relevant KV caches for answer generation. Experiments on long-streaming VideoQA benchmarks show that MuKV significantly improves answer accuracy, without sacrificing memory and online QA efficiency. Moreover, our compression mechanism alone brings consistent benefits across answer accuracy, memory, and QA efficiency over baselines, showcasing highly effective contribution.

</details>


### [62] [Exposing Vulnerabilities in Visible-Infrared VLMs: A Unified Geometric Adversarial Framework with Cross-Task Transferability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22273)
*Xiang Chen,Yuxian Dong,Chao Li,Chengyin Hu,Jiaju Han,Fengyu Zhang,Yiwei Wei,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CFGPatch，一种基于曲线边缘分形几何的对抗补丁框架，用于攻击可见光-红外（VIS-IR）视觉语言模型（VLMs），通过贝塞尔曲线替代直线边缘、模态特定的Fraser螺旋渲染机制以及期望变换（EOT）优化，在跨模态对抗攻击中优于标准补丁基线，并展现出跨任务的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIS-IR感知系统在现实世界中广泛用于恶劣成像条件下的可靠感知，但VLMs在可见光-红外场景下的对抗鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，这一安全缺口亟需填补。

Method: 1) 基于三角形分形几何，用贝塞尔曲线元素替代刚性直线边缘，保留多尺度分形自相似性并引入更平滑的轮廓；2) 设计模态特定的Fraser螺旋渲染机制，向可见光和红外图像注入细粒度纹理失真和误导性感知线索；3) 采用期望变换（EOT）提升对常见图像级变换的鲁棒性。

Result: CFGPatch能有效欺骗VIS-IR VLMs，在攻击有效性和鲁棒性上持续优于标准补丁基线；优化后的对抗样本在零样本分类上可迁移至图像描述和视觉问答任务，展现出强跨任务可迁移性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于曲线分形几何和模态特定纹理干扰的跨模态对抗攻击框架具有显著效果，揭示了VIS-IR VLMs在现实世界部署中的安全隐患。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance across diverse multimodal tasks, but their adversarial robustness in visible-infrared (VIS-IR) scenarios remains underexplored. This gap is critical because VIS-IR sensing is widely used in real-world perception systems to support reliable understanding under challenging imaging conditions. To address this cross-modal threat setting, we propose CFGPatch, a curved-edge fractal geometric adversarial patch framework for attacking VIS-IR VLMs. CFGPatch builds on triangular fractal geometry and replaces rigid straight-edged primitives with Bezier-curved elements, preserving multi-scale fractal self-similarity while introducing smoother contours, richer directional variation, and more flexible shape deformation. In addition, we design a modality-specific Fraser-spiral rendering mechanism to inject fine-grained texture distortions and misleading perceptual cues into visible and infrared images. By coupling global curved-fractal geometry with local spiral-based appearance interference, CFGPatch disrupts both shape perception and texture interpretation. We further adopt expectation over transformation (EOT) to improve robustness against common image-level transformations. Extensive experiments show that CFGPatch effectively fools VIS-IR VLMs and consistently outperforms standard patch baselines in attack effectiveness and robustness. Moreover, adversarial samples optimized for zero-shot classification transfer well to image captioning and visual question answering, demonstrating strong cross-task transferability and generalizability across downstream tasks.

</details>


### [63] [PIU: Proximity-guided Identity Unlearning in ID-Conditioned Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22311)
*Jose Edgar Hernandez Cancino Estrada,Mauro Díaz Lupone,Žiga Emeršič,Vitomir Štruc,Peter Peer,Darian Tomašević*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向身份条件扩散模型（Arc2Face）的身份遗忘框架PIU，通过锚点引导的身份替换目标和邻近性锚点选择策略，仅微调少量跨注意力层即可有效抑制目标身份生成，同时保持其他身份的一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份条件扩散模型能生成高质量且身份一致的人脸，但存在严重的隐私隐患——模型在被要求遗忘后仍可能生成特定个体的图像。现有机器遗忘研究多聚焦于概念或数据删除，面向身份嵌入（而非文本提示）的身份遗忘问题尚属空白。

Method: 提出Proximity-guided Identity Unlearning (PIU)框架：1) 将身份移除建模为身份替换目标，在学习的身份空间中将源身份重新分配给选定的锚点身份；2) 基于ArcFace表示的几何特性设计邻近性锚点选择策略；3) 仅对身份敏感的跨注意力层进行局部微调即可实现有效遗忘。

Result: 在多个目标身份上的实验表明，PIU框架能有效抑制目标身份生成，同时在遗忘指标和图像质量指标上均有提升，保留身份的真实性和一致性也得到了保持。

Conclusion: PIU框架通过锚点引导的身份遗忘策略和局部参数微调，首次系统解决了身份条件扩散模型中的身份遗忘问题，为隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Identity-conditioned diffusion models enable high-quality and identity-consistent face generation, but they also raise severe privacy concerns, as models may continue to synthesize individuals despite their right to be forgotten. While machine unlearning has been extensively studied for concept and data removal, identity unlearning remains largely unexplored, particularly in models conditioned directly on identity embeddings rather than text prompts. In this work, we study identity unlearning in Arc2Face, a state-of-the-art identity-conditioned latent diffusion model for face generation, and introduce Proximity-guided Identity Unlearning (PIU), an anchor-guided framework for identity unlearning. Specifically, we formulate identity removal as an identity replacement objective that reassigns the source identity to a selected anchor identity in the learned identity space, and we complement it with a proximity-based anchor selection strategy motivated by the geometry of ArcFace representations. We further show that effective unlearning can be achieved through localized fine-tuning of a small subset of identity-sensitive cross-attention layers. Experiments across many target identities show that our framework effectively suppresses generation of the target identity while preserving realism and identity consistency for retained identities, as validated by improved performance on unlearning and image-quality metrics, together with qualitative evaluation. The source code for the PIU framework is publicly available at https://github.com/edgarcancinoe/piu_unlearning .

</details>


### [64] [Robustness of breast lesion segmentation under MRI undersampling improves with k-space-aware deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22327)
*Lukas T. Rotkopf,Marco Schlimbach,Julius C. Holzschuh,Heinz-Peter Schlemmer,Jens Kleesiek,Moritz Rempe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究能否直接从采集的MRI k空间学习乳腺病变分割，以及这样做是否能在数据加速或含噪时提升鲁棒性。实验表明，混合k空间-图像模型在欠采样和噪声下显著优于纯图像空间基线，且全采样时性能相当。k空间感知的深度学习在MRI欠采样和噪声下能提升乳腺病变分割的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究直接从k空间学习乳腺病变分割的可行性，并验证该方法在数据加速或添加噪声时是否能提升分割鲁棒性。

Method: 使用公开乳腺DCE-MRI数据集，比较四种3D U-Net变体：混合k空间-图像模型、原生k空间模型、幅度图像空间基线和复数图像空间基线。在递增欠采样和添加复数高斯k空间噪声条件下进行评估，主要指标为患者级Dice相似系数。

Result: 全采样时混合模型与图像空间模型性能相似；随加速增加，混合模型保留更多分割精度，在中等至高欠采样水平显著优于幅度图像空间基线。加噪实验中混合模型退化更慢，图像空间基线在重噪声下失效。特征分析显示k空间和图像空间阶段起互补作用，频域滤波先于图像域病灶定位。

Conclusion: k空间感知的深度学习在MRI欠采样和k空间噪声下能提升乳腺病变分割的鲁棒性，同时在全采样时保持与图像空间方法相当的性能。

Abstract: Purpose: To assess whether breast lesion segmentation can be learned directly from acquired MRI k-space, and whether doing so improves robustness when data are accelerated or noisy.
  Materials and Methods: This retrospective study used public breast dynamic contrast-enhanced MRI (DCE-MRI) datasets with acquired and synthetic k-space, together with a within-dataset synthetic control. We compared four 3D U-Net variants: a hybrid k-space-to-image model, a native k-space model, and magnitude and complex image-space baselines. Models were evaluated under increasing undersampling and added complex Gaussian k-space noise. The primary outcome was patient-level Dice similarity coefficient under cross-validation, with the hybrid model prespecified as the main comparison against the magnitude image-space baseline.
  Results: At full sampling, the hybrid and image-space models performed similarly. As acceleration increased, the hybrid model retained substantially more segmentation accuracy and significantly outperformed the magnitude image-space baseline across moderate to high undersampling levels. The same pattern was observed when noise was added directly to k-space: the hybrid model degraded more slowly, whereas the image-space baseline failed under heavier noise. This advantage was reproduced in the within-dataset synthetic control. Feature analysis suggested that the k-space stage and image-space stage played complementary roles, with frequency-domain filtering concentrated before image-domain lesion localization.
  Conclusion: K-space-aware deep learning improves the robustness of breast lesion segmentation under MRI undersampling and k-space noise, while matching image-space methods at full sampling.

</details>


### [65] [3D LULC classification using multispectral LiDAR and deep learning: current and prospective schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22328)
*Narges Takhtkeshha,Aldino Rizaldy,Markus Hollaus,Juha Hyyppä,Fabio Remondino,Gottfried Mandlburger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了与国家测绘机构对齐的L1和L2土地利用/覆盖分类方案及新的多光谱LiDAR基准数据集，评估了7种深度学习模型，发现Point Transformer V3在双波长LiDAR系统上表现最优（L1 mIoU 79.4%，L2 mIoU 58.9%），且多光谱信息相比仅几何输入分别提升1.1和7.8个百分点，证明了LiDAR反射率在细粒度材料区分中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏公开的、与国家测绘机构分类方案对齐的城市和郊区多光谱LiDAR数据集，限制了深度学习在多光谱LiDAR点云语义分割中的应用。

Method: 提出了L1（8类）和L2（20类）两层次NMCA对齐分类方案，构建了Loosdorf-MSL基准数据集，使用双波长（532 nm和1064 nm）MS LiDAR系统，评估了7种SOTA深度学习模型并进行了光谱消融实验。

Result: Point Transformer V3表现最佳，L1 mIoU为79.4%，L2 mIoU为58.9%；多光谱信息相比仅几何输入在L1提升1.1个百分点，在L2提升7.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 多光谱LiDAR反射率对细粒度材料区分具有重要价值，有助于推动NMCA土地利用/覆盖分类方案向更高语义细节发展，Loosdorf-MSL数据集为一致的国家和国际LULC制图提供了新基准。

Abstract: Land Use Land Cover (LULC) classification is essential for national 3D mapping, geospatial analysis, and sustainable planning. Multispectral (MS) LiDAR provides synchronized spatial-spectral information, and deep learning (DL) enables 3D point cloud semantic segmentation; however, adoption is limited by the lack of publicly available urban and suburban MS LiDAR datasets aligned with National Mapping and Cadastral Agencies (NMCAs) classification schemes. This study addresses these gaps by introducing L1 and L2 NMCA-aligned LULC classification schemes and a new benchmark MS LiDAR dataset. We evaluate seven state-of-the-art DL models and perform spectral ablation studies at both levels of detail. Results show that Point Transformer V3 achieves the best performance, with mIoU of 79.4% (L1, 8 classes) and 58.9% (L2, 20 classes) using a dual-wavelength LiDAR system (532 nm and 1064 nm). Ablation results show that multispectral information improves performance over geometry-only inputs, with gains of 1.1 percentage points at L1 and 7.8 points at L2. These results highlight the value of LiDAR reflectance for fine-grained material discrimination and support the evolution of NMCA LULC schemes toward higher semantic detail. The Loosdorf-MSL dataset contributes a new benchmark for consistent national and international LULC mapping.

</details>


### [66] [4D-GSW: Kinematic-Aware Spatio-Temporal Consistent Watermarking for 4D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22342)
*Sifan Zhou,Hang Zhang,Yuhang Wang,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4D-GSW，一种运动感知的水印框架，通过STC度量、HMM-MRF能量最小化和各向异性梯度路由，在保持高时空一致性的同时将鲁棒版权信息嵌入4D高斯溅射动态重建中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D高斯溅射在动态重建中表现出色，但其知识产权保护仍是空白。现有隐写技术忽略了底层运动流形，导致非物理伪影（如时间闪烁和FVD崩溃），亟需一种运动感知的水印方案。

Method: 1) 提出时空曲率（STC）度量识别"动态瞬间"，自适应门控水印梯度注入以保护关键运动流形；2) 构建联合HMM-MRF能量最小化模型，同步水印相位于时间轨迹和空间邻域；3) 引入各向异性梯度路由机制，确保水印嵌入与光度重建保真度严格解耦。

Result: 实验表明该方法在鲁棒隐藏水印、抵抗多种攻击、保持高渲染质量和时空一致性方面均优于现有方案。

Conclusion: 4D-GSW通过显式考虑运动轨迹的物理一致性，实现了版权保护与高保真动态重建的平衡，为4D资产安全提供了新范式。

Abstract: While 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) has revolutionized high-fidelity dynamic reconstruction, safeguarding the intellectual property of these assets remains an open challenge. Conventional steganographic techniques often neglect the underlying kinematic manifolds, triggering non-physical artifacts such as severe temporal flickering and "FVD collapse". To address this, we propose \textbf{4D-GSW}, a kinematic-aware watermarking framework designed to embed robust copyright information while preserving high spatio-temporal consistency. Unlike prior 4D steganography that primarily focuses on opacity-guided invisibility, our approach explicitly addresses the physical coherence of motion trajectories. We introduce a \textbf{Spatio-Temporal Curvature (STC)} metric to identify "Dynamic Instants," adaptively gating watermark gradient injection to shield critical motion manifolds from non-physical perturbations. To ensure global coherence across complex deformations, we formulate a joint \textbf{HMM-MRF energy minimization} model that synchronizes watermark phases within both temporal trajectories and spatial neighborhoods. Furthermore, an \textbf{anisotropic gradient routing} mechanism ensures that watermark embedding remains strictly decoupled from photometric reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments have demonstrated the superior performance of our method in robustly hiding watermarks while resisting various attacks and maintaining high rendering quality and spatiotemporal consistency.

</details>


### [67] [Bernini: Latent Semantic Planning for Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22344)
*Bernini Team,Chenchen Liu,Junyi Chen,Lei Li,Lu Chi,Mingzhen Sun,Zhuoying Li,Yi Fu,Ruoyu Guo,Yiheng Wu,Ge Bai,Zehuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Bernini框架，通过MLLM进行语义规划、扩散模型进行像素渲染的分工协作，实现视频生成与编辑的统一框架，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在多模态推理和语义理解上表现优异，扩散模型在图像视频合成上具备照片级真实感，但两类模型各自独立发展。作者认为两者可以通过简单的分工协作统一起来：MLLM负责语义规划，扩散模型负责像素渲染。

Method: 提出Bernini统一框架：(1) MLLM规划器直接在ViT嵌入空间中预测目标语义表示；(2) DiT渲染器基于语义规划、文本特征以及编辑时的源VAE特征进行像素合成；(3) 规划器和渲染器可分开训练，仅需轻量协同训练；(4) 引入Segment-Aware 3D Rotary Positional Embedding (SA-3D RoPE)处理多视觉输入；(5) 在规划器中融入链式思维推理以提升理解到生成的迁移能力。

Result: 在多个视频生成和编辑基准上达到最先进性能，MLLM的预训练理解能力在具有挑战性的编辑任务上展现出强泛化性。

Conclusion: MLLM与扩散模型通过语义为接口的分工协作可以实现高效统一的视频生成与编辑框架，分开训练+轻量协同训练的策略既保留了各自预训练优势又保持了训练效率。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and diffusion models have each reached remarkable maturity: MLLMs excel at reasoning over heterogeneous multimodal inputs with strong semantic grounding, while diffusion models synthesize images and videos with photorealistic fidelity. We argue that these two families can be unified through a simple division of labor: MLLMs perform semantic planning, while diffusion models render pixels from high-level semantic guidance and low-level visual features. Building on this idea, we propose Bernini, a unified framework for video generation and editing. An MLLM-based planner predicts the target semantic representation directly in the ViT embedding space, and a DiT-based renderer synthesizes pixels conditioned on this plan, augmented by text features and, for editing, source VAE features for detail preservation. Because semantics serve as the interface, the planner and renderer can be trained separately and only lightly co-trained, preserving the pretrained strengths of both components while keeping training efficient. To better handle multiple visual inputs, we introduce Segment-Aware 3D Rotary Positional Embedding (SA-3D RoPE), and further incorporate chain-of-thought reasoning in the planner to better transfer understanding into generation. Bernini achieves state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of video generation and editing benchmarks, with the MLLM's pretrained understanding translating into strong generalization on challenging editing tasks.

</details>


### [68] [VEELA: A Clinically-Constrained Benchmark for Liver Vessel Segmentation in Computed Tomography Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22357)
*Ziya Ata Yazıcı,N. Sinem Gezer,İlkay Öksüz,İlker Özgür Koska,Tuğçe Toprak,Pervin Bulucu,Ufuk Beşenk,A. Emre Kavur,Pierre-Henri Conze,Hazım Kemal Ekenel,Oğuz Dicle,Mustafa Ege Şeker,Mustafa Said Kartal,Ariorad Moniri,Orhan Özkan,Osman Faruk Bayram,Hakan Polat,Musa Balcı,Ece Tuğba Cebeci,Baran Cılga,Kardelen Peçenek,M. Alper Selver*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VEELA数据集，源于CHAOS挑战的40例CTA扫描，采用严格可见性驱动的多专家逐层标注策略，建立了肝血管分割基准框架，并通过多维度评估指标（clDice、IoU、NSD、面积、长度）揭示了不同指标对血管完整性评估的差异，强调临床相关评估需要多视角分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集缺乏临床真实的标注约束，且肝血管分割因复杂的血管拓扑结构、外周可见性限制及采集伪影而面临挑战，亟需一个严格标注的高质量数据集和标准化评估框架。

Method: 从CHAOS挑战队列中选取40例CTA扫描，采用多专家共识逐层手动标注，执行严格可见性驱动的标注策略，避免解剖推断插值；构建包含拓扑感知(clDice)、重叠率(IoU)、边界敏感(NSD)及几何感知(面积、长度)的多维度评估框架。

Result: 不同评估指标捕捉了血管完整性的不同方面，展示了多视角评估对于临床意义血管分割的必要性；数据集已公开，提供评估指标、数据集和提交平台。

Conclusion: VEELA为肝血管分割提供了严格标注的高质量基准数据集，多维度评估框架有助于推动鲁棒血管分割方法的开发，并促进可重复研究。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of hepatic and portal vessels in contrast-enhanced computed tomography angiography (CTA) remains challenging due to complex vascular topology, peripheral visibility limitations, and acquisition-induced ambiguities. While existing public datasets offer valuable benchmarks, few include clinically realistic annotation constraints. We introduce VEELA (Vessel Extraction and Extrication for Liver Analysis), a rigorously curated liver vessel dataset derived from 40 CTA scans inherited from the CHAOS grand-challenge cohort. All vessels were manually delineated slice-by-slice under multi-expert consensus, using a strict visibility-driven annotation policy and avoiding anatomically inferred interpolation. This design explicitly captures anatomical variability and imaging-related uncertainty. As a continuation of the CHAOS challenge, VEELA enables reproducible cross-benchmark evaluation while extending the scope to fine-grained hepatic and portal vessel segmentation. We further establish a standardized benchmarking framework and analyze complementary evaluation metrics, including topology-aware (clDice), overlap-based (IoU), boundary-sensitive (NSD), and geometry-aware (area, length) measures. Our results demonstrate that different metrics capture distinct aspects of vascular integrity, underscoring the necessity of multi-perspective evaluation for clinically meaningful vessel segmentation. VEELA is publicly released to facilitate reproducible research and support the development of robust vascular segmentation methods. Researchers can access the evaluation metrics, dataset, and submission platform at https://www.synapse.org/Synapse:syn65471967.

</details>


### [69] [GazePrior: Zero-Shot AR/VR Eye Tracking via Learned 3D Gaze Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22359)
*Corentin Dumery,David Colmenares,Alexander Fix,Pascal Fua,Ali Behrooz,Jogendra Kundu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GazePrior模型，利用数据驱动的3D先验分布，通过稀疏输入3D重建将已有眼动追踪设备采集的标注数据渲染到新设备视角，实现无需额外数据采集的高质量合成数据生成，在零样本眼动追踪任务中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 眼动追踪是AR/VR的关键技术，但为每个新设备训练ET模型面临数据采集成本高、耗时长的问题，而现有合成数据方法缺乏真实感，亟需一种无需额外数据收集且能保持数据质量的方法。

Method: 提出GazePrior数据驱动3D先验模型，建模人类眼睛在不同身份、注视方向和光照条件下的分布；利用该先验对已有ET设备采集的标注数据进行稀疏输入3D重建，然后从目标ET设备的相机视角渲染合成数据。

Result: 使用合成数据训练的ET模型在准确性和鲁棒性上显著优于现有的零样本方法。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了真实数据采集的真实感、多样性和标注精度，同时避免了高昂成本，为跨设备眼动追踪提供了一种高效数据生成方案。

Abstract: Eye tracking (ET) is a foundational technology for advanced AR/VR applications. However, training ET models for every new ET device is challenging: real data collection is costly and time-consuming, while existing synthetic data generation methods lack realism. To remove the need for additional data collection while maintaining data quality, we introduce a data-driven 3D prior that models the distribution of human eyes across diverse identities, gaze directions, and light settings. This model, which we coin GazePrior, then enables sparse-input 3D reconstruction of annotated data collected with previous ET devices, which can in turn be rendered from the cameras of any target ET device. Our approach synthesizes data with the realism, diversity and ground-truth accuracy of real data collection without its prohibitive costs. Our experiments demonstrate that ET models trained with our synthesized data outperform previous zero-shot methods, achieving higher accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [70] [AgroTools: A Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Multimodal Agents in Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22366)
*Zi Ye,Yibin Wen,Xiaoya Fan,Xinyu Zhang,Jing Wu,Kun Zeng,Zurong Mai,Jiarui Zhang,Bohan Shi,Juepeng Zheng,Jianxi Huang,Yutong Lu,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgroTools是一个用于评估农业领域工具增强型多模态智能体的基准测试，包含539个问答实例、1,097张农业图像、14个农业工具的执行环境，支持过程级和结果级双视角评估。在9个开源和4个闭源多模态大模型上的测试表明，当前模型在农业工具使用场景中仍不可靠，存在工具规划、参数生成、执行恢复和答案合成等方面的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业多模态基准主要评估最终答案正确性，缺乏对模型使用外部工具完成精度敏感工作流程的能力评估。

Method: 构建了包含539个问答实例、1,097张异构农业图像、14个农业工具执行环境的基准测试，每个查询标注了结构化工具使用轨迹，支持过程级执行质量和结果级任务成功的双视角评估。

Result: 在9个开源和4个闭源多模态大模型上的测试显示，当前模型在农业工具使用场景中远未达到可靠水平，在工具规划、参数生成、执行恢复和最终答案合成方面存在明显瓶颈。

Conclusion: AgroTools可支持未来面向高精度农业应用的多模态智能体研究。

Abstract: Agricultural decision-making increasingly requires multimodal systems that can transform visual observations into reliable, executable actions. However, existing agricultural multimodal benchmarks mainly evaluate final-answer correctness and provide limited support for assessing whether models can use external tools to complete precision-sensitive workflows. In this paper, we introduce AgroTools, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented multimodal agents in agriculture. AgroTools contains 539 question-answer instances paired with 1,097 heterogeneous agricultural images, spanning five task families and an executable environment of 14 agricultural tools. Each query is annotated with structured tool-use traces, enabling a dual-view evaluation of both process-level execution quality and outcome-level task success. We benchmark 9 open-source and 4 closed-source multimodal large language models on AgroTools. Results show that current models remain far from reliable in agricultural tool-use settings, with clear bottlenecks in tool planning, argument generation, execution recovery, and final-answer synthesis. We hope AgroTools will support future research on multimodal agents for high-precision agricultural applications. The benchmark and evaluation are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/AgroTools/AgroTools.

</details>


### [71] [Translating Signals to Languages for sEMG-Based Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22403)
*Ming Wang,Haoxuan Qu,Qiuhong Ke,Wei Zhou,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LLM-sEMG框架，通过将连续sEMG信号转换为“sEMG语言”，利用大语言模型的推理能力实现高精度活动识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言处理中展现出强大的泛化和推理能力，其从大量动作语言描述中学到的隐式知识为解读sEMG信号、推断活动意图提供了新可能。

Method: 设计了一种面向语言的映射机制，将连续sEMG序列转换为sEMG语言，并整合多种策略以促进信号到语言的映射过程，最终利用LLM作为sEMG活动识别器。

Result: 大量实验表明，该框架利用大语言模型实现了高精度的sEMG信号活动识别。

Conclusion: LLM-sEMG框架借助LLM的隐式知识和对动作的语言理解能力，有效解决了sEMG活动识别任务。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) signal-based activity recognition has attracted increasing research attention in recent years. To develop accurate sEMG signal-based activity recognizers, numerous approaches have been proposed. Some studies focus on designing larger and more expressive model architectures to enhance the representational capacity of sEMG signals, while others aim to enrich model priors through large-scale pretraining, thereby improving recognition performance. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable generalization and reasoning capabilities in natural language processing, whose implicit knowledge, learned from extensive linguistic descriptions of actions, opens new possibilities for interpreting sEMG signals and inferring activity intentions. Motivated by this, we propose LLM-sEMG, a novel framework that leverages LLMs as sEMG activity recognizers. Within this framework, we design a language-oriented mapping mechanism that converts continuous sEMG sequences into sEMG language, integrating several strategies to further facilitate the signal-to-language mapping process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves highly accurate sEMG signal-based activity recognition using large language models.

</details>


### [72] [From Recognition to Reasoning: Benchmarking and Enhancing MLLMs on Real-World Receipt Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22413)
*Yandi Wang,Libin Zhan,Ziwei Huang,Tiancheng Luo,Yuxuan Jiang,Wang Dong,Leilei Gan,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReceiptBench——一个包含10,000张人工标注收据的大规模视觉信息抽取基准，将任务划分为四个层次子任务（基础感知、格式规范化、语义推理、结构解析），并提出了基于度量感知GRPO的两阶段训练框架，在复杂推理任务上超越领先的专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIE基准在规模、真实性和语义粒度上存在严重不足，且未能覆盖多样化的文档类型，需要更全面、更具挑战性的基准来推动多模态大模型在结构化信息提取领域的发展。

Method: 构建了10k多样收据的人工标注基准ReceiptBench，将信息抽取分解为四个递进式子任务；提出两阶段训练框架，包含度量感知的GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization），将严格的评估约束转化为强化学习信号以增强结构一致性。

Result: 所提方法在复杂推理任务上取得最先进性能，超越领先的专有模型。

Conclusion: ReceiptBench是一个大规模的、分层次、细粒度的VIE基准，结合度量感知GRPO训练框架能有效提升多模态大模型在结构化信息提取任务上的表现，特别是在复杂推理和结构一致性方面。
